// FUNC 180015c54 ??2@YAPEAX_K@Z
void *__fastcall operator new(size_t Size)
{
  size_t i; // rbx
  void *result; // rax

  for ( i = Size; ; Size = i )
  {
    result = malloc(Size);
    if ( result != nullptr )
      break;
    if ( callnewh(i) == 0 )
    {
      if ( i != -1 )
        __scrt_throw_std_bad_alloc();
      sub_180016718();
    }
  }
  return result;
}

// FUNC 180015c98 _Init_thread_abort
void __fastcall Init_thread_abort(_DWORD *a1)
{
  AcquireSRWLockExclusive(&g_tss_srw);
  *a1 = 0;
  ReleaseSRWLockExclusive(&g_tss_srw);
  WakeAllConditionVariable(&g_tss_cv);
}
// ENDFUNC
// FUNC 180015cd4 _Init_thread_footer
void __fastcall Init_thread_footer(_DWORD *a1)
{
  __int64 v2; // rdx

  AcquireSRWLockExclusive(&g_tss_srw);
  v2 = (unsigned int)TlsIndex;
  *a1 = ++Init_global_epoch;
  *(_DWORD *)(*((_QWORD *)NtCurrentTeb()->ThreadLocalStoragePointer + v2) + 4LL) = Init_global_epoch;
  ReleaseSRWLockExclusive(&g_tss_srw);
  WakeAllConditionVariable(&g_tss_cv);
}
// ENDFUNC
// FUNC 180015d40 _Init_thread_header
void __fastcall Init_thread_header(_DWORD *a1)
{
  AcquireSRWLockExclusive(&g_tss_srw);
